## What is actually implemented (still no native Iced DND controls)

OperationJobs exports PreparedDrag/PreparedDrop/DropChoice/DropTarget/DropActions
from new operation_jobs/drag_drop.rs. prepare_drag(paths, cancellation) captures
original Storage in memory at call, then source observations/retention on queue
worker. One LocalActivity Work hold keeps gesture alive and excludes device
removal. Clones share READY/SUBMITTED/CANCELLED state. cancel invalidates all
plans and releases hold; cannot cancel a transfer already admitted elsewhere.
prepare_drop captures destination storage at call and optional original browser
Source binding; resolves/checks on worker, requires absolute path/binding and
containment. Each target keeps its hover Cancellation, so stale choices refuse
while another target stays usable. choose(preference, offered) is memory-only;
forced unsupported action refuses, Automatic can fall back to Copy. submit_drop
accepts one opaque choice into existing queue/retention/results/conflicts/Undo.

DestinationProof pins the original resolved directory using bounded ObservedSources
(the same FD budget as selected sources). New require_directory validates the
opened directory. Job retains/clones proof into transfer::run; checked on every
DestinationFolder access, rejecting the same path reused by a replacement object.
Finished jobs release proof; stopped jobs retain it. Storage/alias/device proofs
remain original. No new serialized authority/replay permission is introduced.
Generic original namespace/journal/restart/Retry/Continue audits remain open.

Private ClipboardPaste claim is reused without any native clipboard publication.
PreparedSelection.claim(runtime) rejects foreign runtime before consuming a Cut;
wording preserves `another operation history` and `another runtime` assertions.
PreparedTransfer private struct carries optional destination snapshot/proof.
Ordinary clipboard/recovery use None. Deferred cancelled PrepareDrag/PrepareDrop/
Drop controls retire while removal is still active (like cancelled Recovery).
Native Iced/Wayland code is NOT connected to any of this yet.

Native investigation: Winit locked Wayland backend has NO data-device handlers.
Existing file_clipboard/wayland uses ext_data_control on an independent connection,
which cannot start DND on Iced surfaces. Winit must use actual same-connection
source surface and active left implicit-grab serial, not xdg activation's recent
input token. Public window::run only exposes HasWindow/DisplayHandle; avoid
unsafe foreign-proxy tricks/universal command buses. Specific native exchange
window operation/handle in root Iced runtime/Winit fork is a plausible seam.
SCTK0.19.2 has DataDeviceManagerState and DataDeviceHandler/DataOfferHandler/
DataSourceHandler +delegate_data_device!, exact source path in earlier notes.
Read docs/vendor-maintenance.md before fork changes and maintain provenance and
reviewable patches with licenses, one copy only. Layer adapter must explicitly
return unsupported, preserving other apps. Native manager/device binding should
be opt-in rather than altering every ordinary/layer window's clipboard behavior.

Official Wayland spec read (primary source):
https://wayland.freedesktop.org/docs/html/apa.html
Source set_actions once before start_drag; honor LAST negotiated action at drop;
finish only after committed success, otherwise cancel/destroy. File URI receivers
own mutations; NEVER delete source again on generic Move completion. URI16MiB,
read/check5s, off-UI bounded pipes/workers still required. Selection.encode/decode
URIS already preserves raw bytes and refuses remote/malformed references.

Integration cautions to solve through actual native probes, not hypothetical
API layering: existing Iced text clipboard may own its own data device; verify
coexistence using independent GTK peer. Keep generic native offer/source IDs and
callbacks separate from file policy; preserve dropped state through Leave and
coalescing, don't lose Drop/Finish on notice saturation. Multiple protocol sources
can await completion after their implicit grabs end; don't clear a newer source
when an older callback settles. Bound sources, offers and pipe writes/reads.

Source lifecycle notices must survive origin window closure: once dropped into
an external receiver, native Source/URI service may still await its completion
with zero windows; a per-window subscription that vanishes on Closed can leak
backend holds. Before actual drop, Escape/close cancels shared PreparedDrag so
late releases refuse. After own queue admission, source end cannot cancel it.
For internal same-client offers, identify the actual local source/seat in native
state and reuse its opaque PreparedDrag rather than reobserving URI names; do
not trust a public MIME marker as authority.

Iced row_state keyed wrappers preserve identity, listing_ui mouse_area currently
Selects on press and can collapse multi-selection before threshold. Capture it
before child handling; don't discard prepared original selection just because
that click changed current selection. Guard actual origin tab/navigation/epoch
and gesture before starting; a late result after release must cancel/drop its
hold. Native DND Motion supplies pointer coordinates while normal motion is
grabbed. Hit-test real layout/viewport through widget operations/custom Any data
for columns, folder rows and sidebar (folder priority), keep correct native vs
Iced logical scale, and overlay chosen-action feedback without shifting rows.
Modifier-only updates use backend choose; final action must follow negotiation.
Use actual external GTK URI peer and private compositor early, then full Iced
acceptance in dark100/light125, real shared Undo and zero-window transfer lifetime.

## Current new implementation (native Iced DND remains unconnected)

New src/operation_jobs/drag_drop.rs exports PreparedDrag, PreparedDrop, DropChoice,
DropTarget, DropActions through operation_jobs. OperationJobs.prepare_drag(paths,
cancel) captures prepared Storage in memory at invocation, then original selected
entry observations + retention on worker, with a gesture LocalActivity Work hold.
PreparedDrag clones share one READY/SUBMITTED/CANCELLED atomic state; cancel ends
all targets and releases hold. It cannot cancel an already admitted operation.
prepare_drop captures destination Storage before waiting, optional original
browser Source binding (must be absolute and contain target), then checks the
folder and prepares default filesystem action off-caller. Hover Cancellation
stays with the prepared target. choose(preference, offered) is memory-only,
produces opaque DropChoice; forced unsupported action refuses, Automatic may
fall back to Copy. submit_drop accepts only one choice per original gesture and
uses original selection, source placement, original destination placement,
existing queue/retention/conflicts/results/attention/Undo. No Serde on authority.

Critical last fix: DestinationProof retains the resolved original directory with
ObservedSources pins (same bounded FD budget). require_directory checks the
original opened entry is a directory. Job holds/clones proof into transfer::run,
which checks it before every destination-access check. Finished jobs release it;
stopped jobs retain it. No saved row can reconstruct this proof. Job initializers
in operation_jobs/recovery.rs use None for ordinary Recovery. No vendor, dependency,
Cargo or Iced source changes in this stage yet.

Private ClipboardPaste source claim is reused (no native clipboard publication).
PreparedSelection.claim(runtime) now explicitly rejects another runtime before
changing a Cut claim. Error wording retains `another operation history` and
`another runtime` for existing public assertions. PreparedTransfer private struct
carries optional original destination snapshot/proof. Existing Clipboard uses
None and its established source observation/lease. Pending cancelled PrepareDrag,
PrepareDrop or Drop controls retire while removal still owns admission, following
existing Recovery cancellation responsiveness. Existing Recovery wording retained.

## Native research / intended next seam (not implemented)

Locked vendor Winit has NO Wayland data-device handlers; only generic dropped-file
conversion for platforms that emit it. Existing app file_clipboard/wayland uses
ext_data_control on a second connection and cannot start DND on Iced's surfaces.
Official primary Wayland spec read:
https://wayland.freedesktop.org/docs/html/apa.html
start_drag requires the source's active implicit-grab serial and origin surface
on that same client connection. Source actions set once before start; compositor's
last selected action at drop must be honored; native finish only after successful
accepted transfer, otherwise cancel/destroy. File-URI receivers mutate; source
must never delete again. Incoming URI limit16MiB, read/check deadline5s. Existing
Selection.encode/decode(URIS) preserves raw bytes and rejects non-local paths.

Read docs/vendor-maintenance.md before any fork change (already read this turn,
but revalidate if context lost). All shared forks ONLY vendor/, root provenance
and patches together, exact upstream attributions retained; no new copy in Files.
Existing vendor winit and iced runtime/winit have focus/transient extensions.
Candidate native seam: Winit owns optional SCTK DataDeviceManagerState on its
same event loop/connection, tracks actual left press/release serial/seat per
origin (independent of xdg_activation), publishes opaque offers/source lifecycles.
Expose through a specific renderer-neutral Iced runtime window operation/handle;
Iced adapter wraps native callbacks into bounded async notices, no file policy.
Native fd pipe I/O remains off UI and bounded. An extra independent clipboard
connection is insufficient. No code for this seam has been written yet.

SCTK exact0.19.2 files at:
/home/kevinard/.local/share/cargo/registry/src/index.crates.io-1949cf8c6b5b557f/smithay-client-toolkit-0.19.2/src/data_device_manager/
plus examples/data_device.rs. DataDeviceHandler enter/leave/motion/drop,
DataOfferHandler, DataSourceHandler and delegate_data_device! provide real native
wrappers; inspect exact methods rather than guessing. Winit platform Wayland
Window has queue_handle, underlying SctkWindow, event-loop Ping. Existing focus
Input is tied to xdg activation and is not a DND implicit-grab substitute.

Iced runtime window::run(id, closure) exposes only HasWindow/DisplayHandle, not
native Winit downcasting; avoid unsafe foreign display/surface tricks or a
universal command bus. A specific native exchange operation is preferable.
Iced row_state custom widgets already preserve keyed row identity. listings use
mouse_area on_press Select, which collapses multi-selection before threshold;
new source wrapper must capture original selection before that behavior. DND
pointer position comes from data-device Motion (ordinary mouse motion is grabbed).
Targets can be found via a widget Operation/custom Any data + layout bounds for
current/ancestor columns, folder rows and sidebar, with folder priority, captured
Window/Tab/revision/epoch and cancellation. Feedback overlays body without moving
rows; modifier-only changes recompute backend choice without filesystem reads.

publication still before historyopen and returns the SAME ArcStorage. Memory-only

## Implementation ownership

ownedMainContext commandactor. Output preserves observationv2 and adds command
v1 events. command_wire.rs private typedRequest/Command/Answer/Question/Update/

## Implemented backend contract

RequestRecover, RequestReconcile, bounded/cancellable async recovery_page,
RecoveryReport/RecoveryActions, semantic QuestionRecovery/noReplace validation,
shared transfer publication/progress/question code. Retained actual source is
resolved before durable admission; source references and original storage
observations retained. Copy-out uses independent Recovery lane and never replays
primary journal. Ordinary occupied/stale refusals do not block unrelated work.

Exact Retry/Continue plans remain Job-owned in memory. Atomic engine handoff
moves source refs to successor before consuming old capability; refusals,
missing/replaced output parents, queued cancellation and save failures preserve
actual plan/facts. Serialized reports never recreate executable authority.
SavedRecoveryResult is descriptive; handed_off closes old report afterhandoff.

Reconciliation captures complete Pending bodies from BOTH lanes and exact
recorded temporary-directory-access IDs/bodies (path, identity, parent, mode).
Core compares under writer lock before original captured storage check/recover/
trim. Runtime captures uncertain report IDs with that request. Success wraps only
those reports in SavedReconciled, retaining historical facts, persisted phase and
source requirements; snapshotStopped and no executable continuation. Failed
review never acknowledges them. Checkpoint failure is SaveFailed, queue held;
generic RetrySave persists actual wrapper without re-executing journals.
Specific wrapper-checkpoint failure regression remains optional future coverage;
actual partial Recovery result save failure+RetrySave is verified.

Real fixtures include16MiB actual interrupted Undo quarantine, salvage preserving
newer occupied name, explicit retry refusal/success, normal work+Undo afterward;
actual partial directory copy pause/cancel, exact continuation ownership, failed
permission retry/newer output, rejected Replace/stale answer, explicit KeepBoth,
raw destination and reopen with no replay, finalization error confirmed facts.
Private fixture C compiles Wall/Wextra/Werror, no production hooks/user folders.

## Implemented current slice

Backend Catalog Main/All owns raw-name sorting, warnings, no-create reads and
cancellation. Local-runtime Reader owns coalesced snapshots and GIO observation
on one GLib thread, installs watches before reads, observes existing stores and
prospective writable mounted roots/shared .Trash. Main avoids other mounted
stores. Mount-owner events also refresh the catalog. No idle polling; dropping
view stops reader without cancelling accepted queue operations.

Iced Trash auxiliary window owns raw selection, scroll, bounded row construction
and destination drafts. Delete/MoveToTrash submits to existing OperationJobs;
ShowTrash command/Places/overflow opens it. Restore original/chosen captures the
actual TrashItem, joins original rawbasename to a local folder path or escaped
fileURI, never silently overwrites, shares Undo. Orphans remain visible/copyable
through actual storedURI, Restore disabled. Clipboard adapter has real Browser
and Trash origins. QuickLook reads storedrawpath with original-namehint and
opaque identity/size/mtime version, follows only Trash source and closes with it.
Version grants no execution authority or new persisted fields. Unrelated refresh
preserves preview page/zoom; ordinary edits and same-size/date replacement reload.

Dialogclose returns owner focus only while dialogfocused and sequences focus
beforeclose perADR0021. The native fixture waits exactnativeID retirement and
automaticTrashfocus; a title change can precede realnativewindowclose. Temporary
DEBUG traces removed; failure inventory retained.

## Existing resident/headless host / latest user steering

User's daemon proposal already reflected in PRD, Files ADR0076/rootADR0022.
Application starts configuration,volumes/operations,actions beforeIced. --daemon
starts withoutBrowser/initialGUI and blocks waiting foractivation; laterlaunches
use same owner/state lock/rawbyte localUnixsocket/historywriter. Foreground
unless shell/supervisor backgrounds. --quit releases residency, draining windows,
work, decisions, clipboard and unsaved results. No autostart installed.
Toopenlater requires graphicalsessionenvironment atstart; nodisplayowner remains
headless/refusesBrowser. GUI/backend share process, NO frontendcrashisolation.
local-runtime builds withoutIced. Prior resident49native and debug5.37s first /
1.02–1.21s warm,60–61MiB preGUI/190MiB postwindow summedRSS, shortidleCPU remain
historicalscope only, not releaseperformance proof. Independent firstwindow
startup timeout remains unresolved, including final05 attempt; no retry fix.
